   00313:

   00315:     return { hasError: true, err: error };
   00316:   }

   00319:     console.error("App ErrorBoundary caught:", error, info);
   00320:   }
   00321: 
   00322:   render() {
   00323:     if (this.state.hasError) {

   00325:         this.state.err?.message || String(this.state.err || "Erro desconhecido");

   00329:             minHeight: "100vh",

   00024: export function normalizeAnimal(value) {
   00025:   return String(value || "")
   00026:     .trim()
   00027:     .toUpperCase()
   00028:     .normalize("NFD")
   00029:     .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, "")
   00030:     .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
   00031:     .trim();
   00032: }
   00033: 
   00034: function onlyDigits(s) {
   00035:   return String(s || "").replace(/[^\d]/g, "");
   00036: }
   00037: 
   00038: function pad2(n) {
   00039:   return String(Number(n) || 0).padStart(2, "0");
   00040: }
   00041: 
   00042: function stripTrailingSlashes(s) {
   00043:   return String(s || "").replace(/\/+$/, "");
   00044: }
   00045: 
   00046: function ensureLeadingSlash(s) {
   00047:   const v = String(s || "").trim();
   00048:   if (!v) return "";
   00049:   return v.startsWith("/") ? v : `/${v}`;
   00050: }
   00051: 
   00052: /**
   00053:  * ✅ Base pública do app:
>> 00054:  * - Vite: import.meta.env.BASE_URL (normalmente "/")
   00055:  * - CRA: process.env.PUBLIC_URL
   00056:  * - fallback: ""
   00057:  *
   00058:  * Retorna "" (raiz) ou "/subpasta" (sem barra no final).
   00059:  */
   00060: function getPublicBase() {
   00061:   // Vite (preferência)
   00062:   try {
   00063:     const viteBase =
   00064:       typeof import.meta !== "undefined" &&
   00065:       import.meta.env &&
   00066:       typeof import.meta.env.BASE_URL === "string"
   00067:         ? String(import.meta.env.BASE_URL).trim()
   00068:         : "";
   00069: 
   00070:     // BASE_URL do Vite normalmente vem como "/" ou "/sub/"
   00071:     if (viteBase && viteBase !== "/") {
   00072:       return stripTrailingSlashes(ensureLeadingSlash(viteBase));
   00073:     }
   00074:   } catch {
   00075:     // noop
   00076:   }
   00077: 
   00078:   // CRA
   00079:   const craBase =
   00080:     typeof process !== "undefined" &&
   00081:     process.env &&
   00082:     typeof process.env.PUBLIC_URL === "string"
   00083:       ? String(process.env.PUBLIC_URL).trim()
   00084:       : "";
   00085: 
   00086:   if (craBase && craBase !== "/") {
   00087:     return stripTrailingSlashes(ensureLeadingSlash(craBase));
   00088:   }
   00089: 
   00090:   return "";
   00091: }
   00092: 
   00093: /**
   00094:  * ✅ Converte "qualquer coisa que represente grupo" em número 1..25
   00095:  * Aceita:
   00096:  * - 18
   00097:  * - "18"
   00098:  * - "018"
   00099:  * - "GRUPO 18"
   00100:  * - "Grupo 04"
   00101:  * - "G18"
   00102:  *
   00103:  * Proteção extra:
   00104:  * - evita "GRUPO 1 E 2" virar 12
   00105:  */
   00106: function coerceGrupoNumber(input) {
   00107:   if (input == null) return null;
   00108: 
   00109:   if (typeof input === "number") {
   00110:     const g = Math.trunc(input);
   00111:     if (!Number.isFinite(g)) return null;
   00112:     if (g < 1 || g > 25) return null;
   00113:     return g;
   00114:   }
   00115: 
   00116:   const str = String(input || "").trim();
   00117:   if (!str) return null;
   00118: 
   00119:   // pega o PRIMEIRO match de 1 ou 2 dígitos
   00120:   const m = str.match(/\d{1,2}/);
   00121:   if (!m) return null;
   00122: 
   00123:   const g = Number(m[0]);

   00010: function isYmdStrict(s) {
   00011:   const str = String(s || "").trim();
   00012:   if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(str)) return false;
   00013:   const [y, m, d] = str.split("-").map(Number);
   00014:   const dt = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d));
   00015:   return (
   00016:     dt.getUTCFullYear() === y &&
   00017:     dt.getUTCMonth() === m - 1 &&
   00018:     dt.getUTCDate() === d
   00019:   );
   00020: }
   00021: 
   00022: function shouldDebug() {

   00028:   } catch {}
   00029:

   00034:     if (vite) return vite === "1" || vite.toLowerCase() === "true";
   00035:   } catch {}
   00036: 
   00037:   return false;
   00038: }

   00040: export async function runAuditRJ() {
   00041:   const UF = "RJ";
   00042:   const DEBUG = shouldDebug();
   00043: 
   00044:   console.log("[AUDIT][RJ] Rodando auditoria (bounds)...");
   00045: 
   00046:   try {
   00047:     const bounds = await getKingBoundsByUf({ uf: UF });
   00048: 
   00049:     if (DEBUG) {
   00050:       console.log("[AUDIT][RJ] BOUNDS (raw):", bounds);
   00051:       console.log("[AUDIT][RJ] BOUNDS (json):", JSON.stringify(bounds, null, 2));
   00052:     } else {
   00053:       console.log("[AUDIT][RJ] bounds recebido.");
   00054:     }
   00055: 
   00056:     if (!bounds || typeof bounds !== "object") {
   00057:       console.warn("[AUDIT][RJ] bounds inválido (não é objeto):", bounds);
   00058:       return bounds;
   00059:     }
   00060: 
   00061:     const { minDate, maxDate, days, uf } = bounds;
   00062: 
   00063:     if (uf && String(uf).toUpperCase() !== UF) {
   00064:       console.warn("[AUDIT][RJ] UF retornada diferente:", uf);
   00065:     }
   00066: 
   00067:     if (!minDate || !maxDate) {
   00068:       console.warn("[AUDIT][RJ] minDate/maxDate ausentes:", { minDate, maxDate });
   00069:     } else {
   00070:       if (!isYmdStrict(minDate) || !isYmdStrict(maxDate)) {
   00071:         console.warn(
   00072:           "[AUDIT][RJ] minDate/maxDate não estão em YYYY-MM-DD estrito:",
   00073:           { minDate, maxDate }
   00074:         );
   00075:       }
   00076:     }
   00077: 
   00078:     if (days != null) {
   00079:       const n = Number(days);
   00080:       if (!Number.isFinite(n) || n <= 0) {
   00081:         console.warn("[AUDIT][RJ] days suspeito:", days);
   00082:       }
   00083:     }
   00084: 
   00085:     return bounds;
   00086:   } catch (err) {
   00087:     console.error("[AUDIT][RJ] Falha ao buscar bounds:", err);
   00088:     return null;
   00089:   }
   00090: }

   00038:     String(date.getUTCDate()).padStart(2, "0"),
   00039:   ].join("-");
   00040: }

   00057: function pad2(n) {
   00058:   return String(n).padStart(2, "0");
   00059: }
   00060: function isYMD(s) {
   00061:   return /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(String(s || "").trim());
   00062: }
   00063: function ymdToBR(ymd) {
   00064:   const m = String(ymd || "").trim().match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/);
   00065:   if (!m) return "";
   00066:   return `${m[3]}/${m[2]}/${m[1]}`;
   00067: }
   00068: function ymdToUTCDate(ymd) {
   00069:   const m = String(ymd || "").match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/);
   00070:   if (!m) return null;
   00071:   return new Date(Date.UTC(Number(m[1]), Number(m[2]) - 1, Number(m[3])));
   00072: }
   00073: function addDaysUTC(ymd, days) {
   00074:   const dt = ymdToUTCDate(ymd);
   00075:   if (!dt) return ymd;
   00076:   dt.setUTCDate(dt.getUTCDate() + Number(days || 0));
   00077:   const y = dt.getUTCFullYear();
   00078:   const m = pad2(dt.getUTCMonth() + 1);
   00079:   const d = pad2(dt.getUTCDate());
   00080:   return `${y}-${m}-${d}`;
   00081: }
   00082: function todayYMDLocal() {
   00083:   const d = new Date();
   00084:   return `${d.getFullYear()}-${pad2(d.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad2(d.getDate())}`;
   00085: }
   00086: function sortPTBR(a, b) {
   00087:   return String(a).localeCompare(String(b), "pt-BR", { sensitivity: "base" });
   00088: }
   00089: function isTodos(v) {
   00090:   return String(v || "").trim().toLowerCase() === "todos";
   00091: }
   00092: 
   00093: /**
   00094:  * Normaliza "hora" em formato HH:MM (quando possível)
   00095:  */
   00096: function normalizeHourLike(value) {
   00097:   const s0 = String(value ?? "").trim();
   00098:   if (!s0) return "";
   00099:   const s = s0.replace(/\s+/g, "");
   00100: 
   00101:   const mhx = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})(?:h|hs|hr|hrs)$/i);
   00102:   if (mhx) return `${pad2(mhx[1])}:00`;
   00103: 
   00104:   const mISO = s.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{1,2})(?::(\d{1,2}))?$/);
   00105:   if (mISO) return `${pad2(mISO[1])}:${pad2(mISO[2])}`;
   00106: 
   00107:   const m2 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})$/);

   00001: /*
   00002: ===============================================================================
   00003: Motor Probabilístico de Milhares — V3
   00004: ===============================================================================
   00005: 
   00006: Objetivos:
   00007: 
   00008: - preservar integralmente a centena solicitada;
   00009: - gerar sempre as 10 milhares candidatas;
   00010: - utilizar contexto temporal real;
   00011: - não depender de diversificação artificial;
   00012: - não depender de React;
   00013: - manter resultado determinístico;
>> 00014: - aceitar sorteios completos, e não apenas prêmios achatados.
   00015: 
   00016: Fatores padrão:
   00017: 
   00018: - 30% frequência exata histórica;
   00019: - 18% frequência no horário-alvo;
   00020: - 14% frequência no dia da semana-alvo;
   00021: - 12% frequência na posição-alvo;
   00022: - 12% tendência recente;
   00023: - 10% recência cronológica;
   00024: - 4% força recente do prefixo.
   00025: 
   00026: O prefixo é apenas sinal complementar.
   00027: */
   00028: 
   00029: const DEFAULT_WEIGHTS_V3 = Object.freeze({
   00030:   exactFrequency: 0.30,
   00031:   hourFrequency: 0.18,
   00032:   weekdayFrequency: 0.14,
   00033:   positionFrequency: 0.12,
   00034:   recentTrend: 0.12,
   00035:   chronologicalRecency: 0.10,
   00036:   recentPrefixStrength: 0.04,
   00037: });

   00041: function digitsOnly(value) {
   00042:   return String(value ?? "").replace(/\D+/g, "");
   00043: }
   00044: 
   00045: function safeNumber(value, fallback = 0) {
   00046:   const parsed = Number(value);
   00047: 
   00048:   return Number.isFinite(parsed)
   00049:     ? parsed
   00050:     : fallback;
   00051: }
   00052: 
   00053: function firstValue(object, keys) {
   00054:   for (const key of keys) {
   00055:     const value = object?.[key];
   00056: 
   00057:     if (
   00058:       value !== undefined &&
   00059:       value !== null &&
   00060:       String(value).trim() !== ""
   00061:     ) {
   00062:       return value;
   00063:     }
   00064:   }
   00065: 
   00066:   return "";
   00067: }
   00068: 
   00069: export function normalizeCentena3V3(value) {
   00070:   const digits = digitsOnly(value);
   00071: 
   00072:   if (!digits) return "";
   00073: 
   00074:   return digits.slice(-3).padStart(3, "0");
   00075: }
   00076:

   00490: function normalizeLoteriaKey(v) {
   00491:   const raw = String(v ?? "").trim();
   00492: 
   00493:   if (!raw) return "PT_RIO";
   00494: 
   00495:   const key = raw
   00496:     .toLowerCase()
   00497:     .normalize("NFD")
   00498:     .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, "")
   00499:     .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
   00500:     .trim();
   00501: 
   00502:   if (key === "federal" || key === "fed" || key === "br" || key === "brasil") return "FEDERAL";
   00503:   if (key === "rj" || key === "rio" || key === "pt_rio" || key === "pt-rio") return "PT_RIO";
   00504: 
   00505:   const out = key
   00506:     .toUpperCase()
   00507:     .replace(/[^A-Z0-9]+/g, "_")
   00508:     .replace(/^_+|_+$/g, "");
   00509: 
   00510:   return out || "PT_RIO";
   00511: }

   00513: function getDefaultFloorByUf(ufKey) {

   00519: 
   00520:   if (u === "FEDERAL" || u === "FED" || u === "BR" || u === "BRASIL") {
   00521:     return "2022-02-08";
   00522:   }
   00523: 
   00524:   return "2022-01-01";
   00525: }

   00527: function getTodayYmdSafe() {
   00528:   const ymd = normalizeToYMD_SP(new Date());

   00530:

   00534:   const dd = String(now.getDate()).padStart(2, "0");
   00535:   return `${yyyy}-${mm}-${dd}`;
   00536: }

   00040:   if (!input) return null;
   00041: 
   00042:   // Firestore Timestamp com toDate()
   00043:   if (typeof input === "object" && typeof input.toDate === "function") {
   00044:     const d = input.toDate();
   00045:     if (d instanceof Date && !Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) {

   00065:         d.getUTCDate()
   00066:       )}`;
   00067:     }
   00068:   }
   00069:

   00073:       input.getUTCDate()
   00074:     )}`;
   00075:   }
   00076: 
   00077:   const s = String(input).trim();
   00078:   if (!s) return null;

   00013:  * - Inputs de data NÃO dependem de boundsReady (para o calendário nunca “morrer”).

   00030: 
   00031: function pad2(n) {
   00032:   return String(n).padStart(2, "0");
   00033: }
   00034: 
   00035: function isISODate(s) {
   00036:   return /^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/.test(String(s || ""));
   00037: }
   00038: 
   00039: function formatBR(iso) {
   00040:   if (!isISODate(iso)) return "";
   00041:   const [y, m, d] = iso.split("-");
   00042:   return `${d}/${m}/${y}`;
   00043: }
   00044: 
   00045: function toUTCDate(iso) {
   00046:   if (!isISODate(iso)) return null;
   00047:   const [y, m, d] = iso.split("-").map(Number);
   00048:   if (!y || !m || !d) return null;
   00049:   return new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d));
   00050: }
   00051: 
   00052: function fromUTCDate(dt) {
   00053:   if (!(dt instanceof Date) || !Number.isFinite(dt.getTime())) return null;
   00054:   const y = dt.getUTCFullYear();
   00055:   const m = pad2(dt.getUTCMonth() + 1);
   00056:   const d = pad2(dt.getUTCDate());
   00057:   return `${y}-${m}-${d}`;
   00058: }
   00059: 
   00060: function clamp(n, a, b) {
   00061:   return Math.max(a, Math.min(b, n));
   00062: }
   00063: 
   00064: function clampDateISO(iso, minISO, maxISO) {
   00065:   const dt = toUTCDate(iso);
   00066:   const minDt = toUTCDate(minISO);
   00067:   const maxDt = toUTCDate(maxISO);
   00068: 
   00069:   if (!minDt || !maxDt) return isISODate(iso) ? iso : null;
   00070:   if (!dt) return maxISO;
   00071: 
   00072:   const clamped = new Date(clamp(dt.getTime(), minDt.getTime(), maxDt.getTime()));
   00073:   return fromUTCDate(clamped) || maxISO;
   00074: }
   00075: 
   00076: function daysBetweenUTC(aISO, bISO) {
   00077:   const a = toUTCDate(aISO);
   00078:   const b = toUTCDate(bISO);
   00079:   if (!a || !b) return 0;

   00069: /** Normaliza posição para "1º..7º" ou "Todos" */
   00070: function normalizePosicaoInput(v) {
   00071:   const s = String(v ?? "").trim();
   00072:   if (!s) return "Todos";
   00073:   if (s.toLowerCase() === "todos") return "Todos";
   00074: 
   00075:   const mNum = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})$/);
   00076:   if (mNum) return `${Number(mNum[1])}º`;
   00077: 
   00078:   const mOrd = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})\s*º$/);
   00079:   if (mOrd) return `${Number(mOrd[1])}º`;
   00080: 
   00081:   return s;
   00082: }

   00005:  * KPI cards (top right) — premium executivo (legível e responsivo)

   00007:  * - Se items vier faltando/ruim, calcula via drawsRaw:
>> 00008:  *   - Sorteios = drawsRaw.length
   00009:  *   - Dias = unique dates (robusto)
   00010:  *

   00012:  * - Remove bolinha dourada ao lado do número
   00013:  * - Centraliza TÍTULO + NÚMERO (horizontalmente) dentro do card
   00014:  * - Mantém o “desce levemente” do número para casar com o fim do banner
   00015:  * - Mantém tipografia responsiva com clamp()
   00016:  * - Mantém layout estável sem “explodir” altura
   00017:  *
   00018:  * ✅ NOVO (padrão ChartsGrid):
   00019:  * - drawsRaw = VIEW (filtrado)
   00020:  * - drawsRawGlobal = GLOBAL (sem filtros) [opcional]
   00021:  * - showGlobalAparicoes (default false): adiciona KPI "Aparições (Geral)" baseado em prizes do GLOBAL.
   00022:  */
   00023: 
   00024: function pad2(n) {
   00025:   return String(n).padStart(2, "0");
   00026: }
   00027: 
   00028: /**
   00029:  * ✅ FIX (UTC-safe):
   00030:  * Gera YMD usando getters UTC para evitar "virar o dia" por timezone (-03).
   00031:  */
   00032: function ymdFromDateUTC(d) {
   00033:   return `${d.getUTCFullYear()}-${pad2(d.getUTCMonth() + 1)}-${pad2(d.getUTCDate())}`;
   00034: }
   00035: 
   00036: function normalizeToYMD(input) {
   00037:   if (!input) return null;
   00038: 
   00039:   // Firestore Timestamp-like: { toDate() }
   00040:   if (typeof input === "object" && typeof input.toDate === "function") {
   00041:     const d = input.toDate();
   00042:     if (d instanceof Date && !Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) {
   00043:       return ymdFromDateUTC(d);
   00044:     }
   00045:   }
   00046: 
   00047:   // Timestamp-like: { seconds } / { _seconds }
   00048:   if (
   00049:     typeof input === "object" &&
   00050:     (Number.isFinite(Number(input.seconds)) || Number.isFinite(Number(input._seconds)))
   00051:   ) {
   00052:     const sec = Number.isFinite(Number(input.seconds))
   00053:       ? Number(input.seconds)
   00054:       : Number(input._seconds);
   00055: 
   00056:     const d = new Date(sec * 1000);
   00057:     if (!Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) {
   00058:       return ymdFromDateUTC(d);
   00059:     }
   00060:   }
   00061: 
   00062:   if (input instanceof Date && !Number.isNaN(input.getTime())) {
   00063:     return ymdFromDateUTC(input);
   00064:   }
   00065: 
   00066:   const s = String(input).trim();
   00067:   if (!s) return null;
   00068: 
   00069:   const iso = s.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})/);
   00070:   if (iso) return `${iso[1]}-${iso[2]}-${iso[3]}`;
   00071: 
   00072:   const br = s.match(/^(\d{2})\/(\d{2})\/(\d{4})$/);
   00073:   if (br) return `${br[3]}-${br[2]}-${br[1]}`;
   00074: 
   00075:   return null;
   00076: }
   00077:

   00149: }

   00189: function normalizeUfToQueryKey(input) {
   00190:   const s = safeStr(input).toUpperCase();
   00191:   if (!s) return "";
   00192:   if (s.includes("_") || s.length > 2) return s;
   00193:   return UF_TO_LOTTERY_KEY[s] || s;
   00194: }
   00195: 
   00196: function lotteryLabelFromKey(key) {
   00197:   const s = safeStr(key).toUpperCase();
   00198:   if (s === "PT_RIO") return "RIO";
   00199:   if (s === "FEDERAL") return "FEDERAL";
   00200:   if (s.length === 2) return s;
   00201:   const parts = s.split("_");
   00202:   return parts[parts.length - 1] || s;
   00203: }

>> 00209: function clampYmd(ymd, minDate, maxDate) {
   00210:   const d = normalizeToYMD(ymd);
   00211:   if (!isYMD(d)) return null;
   00212: 
   00213:   let out = d;
   00214: 
   00215:   if (isYMD(minDate) && out < minDate) out = minDate;
   00216:   if (isYMD(maxDate) && out > maxDate) out = maxDate;
   00217: 
   00218:   return out;
   00219: }
   00220: 
   00221: function normalizeRangeWithBounds(fromIn, toIn, minDate, maxDate) {
   00222:   let from = clampYmd(fromIn, minDate, maxDate);
   00223:   let to = clampYmd(toIn, minDate, maxDate);
   00224: 
   00225:   const fallback = clampYmd(todayYMDLocal(), minDate, maxDate) || maxDate || minDate || todayYMDLocal();
   00226: 
   00227:   if (!from) from = fallback;
   00228:   if (!to) to = from;
   00229: 
   00230:   if (from > to) [from, to] = [to, from];
   00231: 
   00232:   return { from, to };
   00233: }

   00028: 
   00029: function pad2(n) {
   00030:   return String(n).padStart(2, "0");
   00031: }
   00032: 
   00033: function safeStr(v) {
   00034:   return String(v ?? "").trim();
   00035: }
   00036: 
   00037: function todayYMDLocal() {
   00038:   const d = new Date();
   00039:   return `${d.getFullYear()}-${pad2(d.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad2(d.getDate())}`;
   00040: }
   00041: 
   00042: function isYMD(s) {
   00043:   return /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(String(s || "").trim());
   00044: }
   00045: 
   00046: function ymdToBR(ymd) {
   00047:   const m = String(ymd || "")
   00048:     .trim()
   00049:     .match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/);
   00050:   if (!m) return "";
   00051:   return `${m[3]}/${m[2]}/${m[1]}`;
   00052: }
   00053: 
   00054: function ymdToUTCDate(ymd) {
   00055:   const m = String(ymd || "").match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/);
   00056:   if (!m) return null;
   00057:   return new Date(Date.UTC(Number(m[1]), Number(m[2]) - 1, Number(m[3])));
   00058: }
   00059: 
   00060: function diffDaysUTC(aYmd, bYmd) {
   00061:   const a = ymdToUTCDate(aYmd);
   00062:   const b = ymdToUTCDate(bYmd);
   00063:   if (!a || !b) return NaN;
   00064:   return Math.floor((b.getTime() - a.getTime()) / 86400000);
   00065: }
   00066: 
   00067: function toGrupo2(n) {
   00068:   const v = Number(n);
   00069:   if (!Number.isFinite(v)) return "";
   00070:   return pad2(v);
   00071: }
   00072: 
   00073: function isValidGrupo(n) {
   00074:   const v = Number(n);
   00075:   return Number.isFinite(v) && v >= 1 && v <= 25;
   00076: }
   00077: 
   00078: function sortPTBR(a, b) {
   00079:   return String(a || "").localeCompare(String(b || ""), "pt-BR", { sensitivity: "base" });
   00080: }

   00396: function normalizeImgSrc(src) {
   00397:   const s = safeStr(src);
   00398:   if (!s) return "";
   00399: 
   00400:   if (/^https?:\/\//i.test(s)) return s;
   00401: 
   00402:   const base = publicBase();
   00403: 
   00404:   if (s.startsWith("/")) return `${base}${s}`;
   00405:   if (s.startsWith("public/")) return `${base}/${s.slice("public/".length)}`;
   00406:   if (s.startsWith("img/")) return `${base}/${s}`;
   00407: 
   00408:   return `${base}/${s}`;
   00409: }

   00411: function makeImgVariantsFromGrupo(grupo, size) {

   00426: 
   00427:   const out = [];

   00433:

   00437:     out.push(clean.replace(/\.(png|PNG)$/i, ".jpg"));
   00438:     out.push(clean.replace(/\.(png|PNG|jpg)$/i, ".jpeg"));
   00439:   }
   00440: 
   00441:   return Array.from(new Set(out.filter(Boolean)));
   00442: }

   00010: /**
   00011:  * Search — Premium (SEM índice)
   00012:  * - 2 dígitos => dezena (últimos 2)  ✅ preserva zero à esquerda
   00013:  * - 3 dígitos => centena (últimos 3) ✅ preserva zero à esquerda
   00014:  * - 4 dígitos => milhar (4)         ✅ preserva zero à esquerda
   00015:  *
   00016:  * ✅ REGRA GLOBAL DO PROJETO:
   00017:  * - 7º prêmio = CENTENA (3 dígitos) e NÃO pode virar 4 dígitos.
   00018:  * - Nenhum zero à esquerda pode ser ignorado.
   00019:  *
   00020:  * ✅ UI:
   00021:  * - Mantém apenas 1 card, com 1 bloco: "OCORRÊNCIAS"
   00022:  *
   00023:  * ✅ PERF:
>> 00024:  * - Busca em chunks maiores (60 dias) + concorrência limitada
   00025:  * - Renderiza resultados de forma incremental
   00026:  */
   00027: 
   00028: function pad2(n) {
   00029:   return String(n).padStart(2, "0");
   00030: }
   00031: 
   00032: function safeStr(v) {
   00033:   return String(v ?? "").trim();
   00034: }
   00035: 
   00036: function isYMD(s) {
   00037:   return /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(String(s || "").trim());
   00038: }
   00039: 
   00040: /* =========================
   00041:    Bounds helpers (robusto)
   00042: ========================= */
   00043: 
   00044: function normalizeBoundsResponse(b) {
   00045:   const minRaw = safeStr(b?.minYmd || b?.minDate || b?.min || "");
   00046:   const maxRaw = safeStr(b?.maxYmd || b?.maxDate || b?.max || "");
   00047:   const minYmd = isYMD(minRaw) ? minRaw : null;
   00048:   const maxYmd = isYMD(maxRaw) ? maxRaw : null;
   00049:   return { minYmd, maxYmd, source: safeStr(b?.source || "") };
   00050: }
   00051: 
   00052: function clampYmd(ymd, minYmd, maxYmd) {
   00053:   const d = safeStr(ymd);
   00054:   if (!isYMD(d)) return "";
   00055:   let out = d;
   00056: 
   00057:   const min = safeStr(minYmd);
   00058:   const max = safeStr(maxYmd);
   00059: 
   00060:   if (isYMD(min) && out < min) out = min;
   00061:   if (isYMD(max) && out > max) out = max;
   00062: 
   00063:   return out;
   00064: }
   00065: 
   00066: function normalizeDigitsOnly(v) {
   00067:   return String(v ?? "").replace(/\D+/g, "");
   00068: }
   00069: 
   00070: /**
   00071:  * ✅ Extrai os ÚLTIMOS N dígitos e preserva zero à esquerda.
   00072:  * - Não converte para Number em nenhum momento.
   00073:  */
   00074: function extractLastNDigits(v, n) {
   00075:   const digits = normalizeDigitsOnly(v);
   00076:   if (!digits) return null;
   00077:   const last = digits.slice(-n).padStart(n, "0");
   00078:   const re = new RegExp(`^\\d{${n}}$`);
   00079:   return re.test(last) ? last : null;
   00080: }
   00081: 
   00082: function normalizeHourLike(value) {

   00015:  *
   00016:  * ✅ REGRA GLOBAL DO PROJETO:
   00017:  * - 7º prêmio = CENTENA (3 dígitos) e NÃO pode virar 4 dígitos.
   00018:  * - Nenhum zero à esquerda pode ser ignorado.

   00028: function pad2(n) {
   00029:   return String(n).padStart(2, "0");
   00030: }
   00031: 
   00032: function safeStr(v) {
   00033:   return String(v ?? "").trim();
   00034: }
   00035: 
   00036: function isYMD(s) {
   00037:   return /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(String(s || "").trim());
   00038: }
   00039: 
   00040: /* =========================

   00044: function normalizeBoundsResponse(b) {
>> 00045:   const minRaw = safeStr(b?.minYmd || b?.minDate || b?.min || "");
   00046:   const maxRaw = safeStr(b?.maxYmd || b?.maxDate || b?.max || "");

   00048:   const maxYmd = isYMD(maxRaw) ? maxRaw : null;
   00049:   return { minYmd, maxYmd, source: safeStr(b?.source || "") };

   00051: 
   00052: function clampYmd(ymd, minYmd, maxYmd) {
   00053:   const d = safeStr(ymd);
   00054:   if (!isYMD(d)) return "";
   00055:   let out = d;
   00056: 
   00057:   const min = safeStr(minYmd);
   00058:   const max = safeStr(maxYmd);
   00059: 
   00060:   if (isYMD(min) && out < min) out = min;
   00061:   if (isYMD(max) && out > max) out = max;
   00062: 
   00063:   return out;
   00064: }
   00065: 
   00066: function normalizeDigitsOnly(v) {
   00067:   return String(v ?? "").replace(/\D+/g, "");
   00068: }
   00069: 
   00070: /**
   00071:  * ✅ Extrai os ÚLTIMOS N dígitos e preserva zero à esquerda.
   00072:  * - Não converte para Number em nenhum momento.
   00073:  */
   00074: function extractLastNDigits(v, n) {
   00075:   const digits = normalizeDigitsOnly(v);
   00076:   if (!digits) return null;
   00077:   const last = digits.slice(-n).padStart(n, "0");
   00078:   const re = new RegExp(`^\\d{${n}}$`);
   00079:   return re.test(last) ? last : null;
   00080: }
   00081: 
   00082: function normalizeHourLike(value) {
   00083:   const s0 = String(value ?? "").trim();
   00084:   if (!s0) return "";
   00085:   const s = s0.replace(/\s+/g, "");
   00086: 
   00087:   const mhx = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})(?:h|hs|hr|hrs)$/i);
   00088:   if (mhx) return `${pad2(mhx[1])}:00`;
   00089: 
   00090:   const mISO = s.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{1,2})(?::(\d{1,2}))?$/);
   00091:   if (mISO) return `${pad2(mISO[1])}:${pad2(mISO[2])}`;
   00092: 
   00093:   const m2 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})$/);
   00094:   if (m2) return `${pad2(m2[1])}:00`;
   00095: 
   00096:   return s0.trim();
   00097: }
   00098: 
   00099: // Bucket "09h", "11h"…
   00100: function toHourBucket(value) {
   00101:   const s = String(value ?? "").trim();
   00102:   if (!s || s.toLowerCase() === "todos") return null;
   00103: 
   00104:   const mh = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})h$/i);
   00105:   if (mh) return `${pad2(mh[1])}h`;
   00106: 
   00107:   const m1 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})$/);
   00108:   if (m1) return `${pad2(m1[1])}h`;
   00109: 
   00110:   const m2 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})$/);
   00111:   if (m2) return `${pad2(m2[1])}h`;
   00112: 
   00113:   const m3 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2}):(\d{2})$/);
   00114:   if (m3) return `${pad2(m3[1])}h`;

   00494: async function runPool(items, worker, concurrency = 3) {
   00495:   const queue = [...items];
   00496:   const runners = Array.from({ length: Math.max(1, concurrency) }, async () => {
   00497:     while (queue.length) {
   00498:       const it = queue.shift();
   00499:       // eslint-disable-next-line no-await-in-loop
   00500:       await worker(it);
   00501:     }
   00502:   });
   00503:   await Promise.all(runners);
   00504: }

   00012: 
   00013: - Entende targetYmd / targetHourBucket
   00014: - Encontra o draw imediatamente anterior ao alvo
   00015: - Separa transições por faixa de horário
   00016: - Evita ordenar sorteio sem hora como 00h
>> 00017: - Evita vazamento usando apenas histórico anterior ao alvo no modo target_aware
   00018: */
   00019: 
   00020: function safeInt(v, fallback = 0) {
   00021:   const n = Number(v);
   00022:   return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : fallback;
   00023: }
   00024: 
   00025: function drawSortTs(draw) {
   00026:   const y = pickDrawYMD(draw);
   00027:   const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));
   00028: 
   00029:   if (!isYMD(y) || !h) return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
   00030: 
   00031:   const mins = hourToInt(h);
   00032:   if (!Number.isFinite(mins) || mins < 0) return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
   00033: 
   00034:   const [Y, M, D] = y.split("-").map(Number);
   00035:   const base = Date.UTC(Y, M - 1, D);
   00036: 
   00037:   return base + mins * 60 * 1000;
   00038: }
   00039: 
   00040: function slotTs(ymd, hourBucket) {
   00041:   const safeYmd = String(ymd || "").trim();
   00042:   const h = toHourBucket(hourBucket);
   00043: 
   00044:   if (!isYMD(safeYmd) || !h) return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
   00045: 
   00046:   const mins = hourToInt(h);
   00047:   if (!Number.isFinite(mins) || mins < 0) return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
   00048: 
   00049:   const [Y, M, D] = safeYmd.split("-").map(Number);
   00050:   const base = Date.UTC(Y, M - 1, D);
   00051: 
   00052:   return base + mins * 60 * 1000;
   00053: }
   00054: 
   00055: function dedupeAndSortDraws(draws) {
   00056:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
   00057:   const map = new Map();
   00058: 
   00059:   for (const d of list) {
   00060:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
   00061:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
   00062:     const ts = drawSortTs(d);
   00063: 
   00064:     if (!Number.isFinite(ts)) continue;
   00065: 
   00066:     const key =
   00067:       String(d?.id || d?.drawId || "").trim() ||
   00068:       (isYMD(y) && h ? `${y}|${h}` : "");
   00069: 
   00070:     if (!key) continue;
   00071: 
   00072:     const prev = map.get(key);
   00073: 
   00074:     if (!prev || ts < drawSortTs(prev)) {
   00075:       map.set(key, d);
   00076:     }
   00077:   }
   00078: 
   00079:   return Array.from(map.values()).sort((a, b) => drawSortTs(a) - drawSortTs(b));
   00080: }
   00081: 
   00082: function makeEdgeKey(fromHour, toHour) {
   00083:   const from = toHourBucket(fromHour);
   00084:   const to = toHourBucket(toHour);
   00085: 
   00086:   if (!from || !to) return "";

========================================================================================================================

   00275: /**
   00276:  * Determina apenas os slots esperados.
>> 00277:  * Nenhum resultado histórico é removido.

   00279: export function getPtRioScheduleForYmd(
   00280:   ymd,
   00281:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
   00282:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT
   00283: ) {
   00284:   const y = safeStr(ymd);
   00285:   const dow = Number(getDowKey(y));
   00286: 
   00287:   if (dow === 0) {
   00288:     const sundaySchedule = Array.isArray(PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_SUNDAY)
   00289:       ? [...PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_SUNDAY]
   00290:       : [];
   00291: 
   00292:     if (y >= PT_RIO_SUNDAY_REDUCED_START_YMD) {
   00293:       return sundaySchedule.filter((hour) => {
   00294:         const h = toHourBucket(hour);
   00295: 
   00296:         return h === "14:00" || h === "16:00";
   00297:       });
   00298:     }
   00299: 
   00300:     return sundaySchedule;
   00301:   }
   00302: 
   00303:   if (dow === 6) {
   00304:     const saturdaySchedule = Array.isArray(
   00305:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT
   00306:     )
   00307:       ? [...PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT]
   00308:       : [];
   00309: 
   00310:     if (y >= "2026-07-18") {
   00311:       const transitionedSchedule = saturdaySchedule
   00312:         .map(toHourBucket)
   00313:         .filter(Boolean)
   00314:         .filter((hour) => hour !== "18:00");
   00315: 
   00316:       if (!transitionedSchedule.includes("19:00")) {
   00317:         transitionedSchedule.push("19:00");
   00318:       }
   00319: 
   00320:       return transitionedSchedule
   00321:         .filter(
   00322:           (hour, index, schedule) =>
   00323:             schedule.indexOf(hour) === index
   00324:         )
   00325:         .sort(
   00326:           (hourA, hourB) =>
   00327:             hourToInt(hourA) - hourToInt(hourB)
   00328:         );
   00329:     }
   00330: 
   00331:     return saturdaySchedule;
   00332:   }
   00333: 
   00334:   if (dow === 3) {
   00335:     return Array.isArray(PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT)
   00336:       ? [...PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT]
   00337:       : [];
   00338:   }
   00339: 
   00340:   return Array.isArray(PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL)
   00341:     ? [...PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL]
   00342:     : [];
   00343: }

   00709: /* =========================
   00710:    Bounds (min/max reais)
   00711: ========================= */
   00712: 
   00713: function pickMinMaxFromMapped(mapped) {
   00714:   let minYmd = null;
   00715:   let maxYmd = null;
   00716: 
   00717:   for (const d of mapped) {
   00718:     const y = d?.ymd || normalizeToYMD(d?.date);
   00719:     if (!y || !isYMD(y)) continue;
   00720:     if (!minYmd || y < minYmd) minYmd = y;
   00721:     if (!maxYmd || y > maxYmd) maxYmd = y;
   00722:   }
   00723: 
   00724:   return { minYmd, maxYmd };
   00725: }
   00726: 
   00727: /**
   00728:  * ✅ “Prova de vida” do maxYmd sem depender de índice composto nem de docId:
>> 00729:  * tenta dias recentes (==) desc até achar pelo menos 1 draw.
   00730:  */
   00731: async function probeRecentMaxYmd(uf, lookbackDays = 45) {
   00732:   const base = utcTodayYmd();
   00733:   const n = Math.max(3, Math.min(120, Number(lookbackDays) || 45));
   00734: 
   00735:   for (let i = 0; i <= n; i += 1) {
   00736:     const day = addDaysUTCLocal(base, -i);

   00753: async function fetchBoundsFromApi(ufOrObj) {

   00763:     if (!r.ok || !j || !j.ok) {
   00764:       return {

   00777:         payload: j,
   00778:       };
   00779:     }

   00789:       source: String(j.source || "api_bounds"),

   00793:   }

   01004: function buildPrizeSignature(draw) {
   01005:   const prizes = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
   01006:   if (!prizes.length) return "";
   01007: 
   01008:   const parts = prizes
   01009:     .map((p) => {
   01010:       const pos = Number(p?.position);
   01011:       const grupo = Number(p?.grupo);
   01012:       const numero = toPrizeDigitsByPosition(
   01013:         p?.numero ?? p?.milhar ?? p?.number ?? p?.num ?? p?.valor ?? "",
   01014:         pos
   01015:       );
   01016: 
   01017:       if (!Number.isFinite(pos) || !Number.isFinite(grupo) || !numero) return "";
   01018:       return `${pad2(pos)}:${pad2(grupo)}:${numero}`;
   01019:     })
   01020:     .filter(Boolean)
   01021:     .sort();
   01022: 
   01023:   return parts.join("|");
   01024: }
   01025: 
   01026: function federalHourPriority(draw) {
   01027:   const h = normalizeHourLike(
   01028:     draw?.close_hour || draw?.closeHour || draw?.hour || draw?.hora || ""
   01029:   );
   01030:   const ymd = String(draw?.ymd || normalizeToYMD(draw?.date) || "").trim();
   01031:   const n = hourToNumSafe(h);
   01032: 
   01033:   const prefer20h = getFederalPreferredHourForYmd(ymd) === "20:00";
   01034: 
   01035:   if (prefer20h) {
   01036:     if (h === "20:00") return 200000 + n;
   01037:     if (h === "19:00") return 190000 + n;
   01038:     return 100000 + n;
   01039:   }
   01040: 
   01041:   if (h === "19:00") return 200000 + n;
   01042:   if (h === "20:00") return 190000 + n;
   01043:   return 100000 + n;
   01044: }

   01046: function pickBetterFederalClone(a, b) {

   01057:     : 0;

   00005: /**
   00006:  * =====================================================
   00007:  * PLAN SERVICE — PALPITACO
   00008:  * =====================================================
   00009:  * Regras do produto:
   00010:  * - Usuário novo: entra FREE + ganha TRIAL 24h automaticamente
>> 00011:  * - Premium: 30 dias após confirmação de pagamento
   00012:  * - Ao expirar premium: volta a FREE automaticamente
   00013:  *
   00014:  * Estrutura sugerida:
   00015:  * users/{uid}:
   00016:  *  - createdAt, updatedAt
   00017:  *  - plan: {
   00018:  *      tier: "free" | "trial" | "premium",
   00019:  *      trialStartMs: number | null,
   00020:  *      premiumUntilMs: number | null,
   00021:  *      lastPayment: {
   00022:  *        amount: number,
   00023:  *        provider: "mercadopago" | "manual" | "other",
   00024:  *        paymentId: string,
   00025:  *        confirmedAtMs: number
   00026:  *      } | null
   00027:  *    }
   00028:  */
   00029: 
   00030: const ONE_HOUR_MS = 60 * 60 * 1000;
   00031: const TRIAL_MS = 24 * ONE_HOUR_MS;
   00032: const PREMIUM_30D_MS = 30 * 24 * ONE_HOUR_MS;
   00033: 
   00034: function nowMs() {
   00035:   return Date.now();
   00036: }
   00037: 
   00038: function safeNum(v) {
   00039:   const n = Number(v);
   00040:   return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : null;
   00041: }
   00042: 
   00043: function normalizeTier(tierRaw) {
   00044:   const t = String(tierRaw ?? "free").trim().toLowerCase();
   00045:   if (t === "premium" || t === "trial") return t;
   00046:   return "free";
   00047: }
   00048: 
   00049: function normalizePlan(plan) {
   00050:   const tier = normalizeTier(plan?.tier);
   00051:   const trialStartMs = safeNum(plan?.trialStartMs);
   00052:   const premiumUntilMs = safeNum(plan?.premiumUntilMs);
   00053: 
   00054:   return {
   00055:     tier,
   00056:     trialStartMs,
   00057:     premiumUntilMs,
   00058:     lastPayment: plan?.lastPayment || null,
   00059:   };
   00060: }
   00061: 
   00062: function normalizeProvider(v) {
   00063:   const s = String(v ?? "").trim().toLowerCase();
   00064:   if (s === "mercadopago" || s === "manual" || s === "other") return s;
   00065:   return "other";
   00066: }
   00067: 
   00068: function normalizePaymentId(v) {
   00069:   const s = String(v ?? "").trim();
   00070:   return s ? s : null;
   00071: }
   00072:

   00180: export async function activatePremium30d(uid, payload = {}) {
   00181:   if (!uid) throw new Error("activatePremium30d(uid): uid ausente");
   00182: 
   00183:   const ref = doc(db, "users", uid);
   00184:   const snap = await getDoc(ref);
   00185:   const t = nowMs();
   00186: 
   00187:   let basePlan = { tier: "free", trialStartMs: null, premiumUntilMs: null, lastPayment: null };

   00190:   }

   00193:     typeof basePlan.premiumUntilMs === "number" ? basePlan.premiumUntilMs : 0;
   00194: 
   00195:   // ✅ Se ainda está premium, soma a partir do vencimento atual; senão soma a partir de agora
   00196:   const startFrom = currentUntil > t ? currentUntil : t;
   00197:   const newUntil = startFrom + PREMIUM_30D_MS;
   00198: 
   00199:   const nextPlan = {
   00200:     ...basePlan,
   00201:     tier: "premium",
   00202:     premiumUntilMs: newUntil,
   00203:     // trial pode existir historicamente, mas não interfere no entitlement (premium manda)
   00204:     lastPayment: {
   00205:       amount: safeNum(payload.amount),
   00206:       provider: normalizeProvider(payload.provider || "mercadopago"),
   00207:       paymentId: normalizePaymentId(payload.paymentId),
   00208:       confirmedAtMs: t,
   00209:     },
   00210:   };
   00211: 
   00212:   if (!snap.exists()) {
   00213:     await setDoc(ref, {
   00214:       createdAt: serverTimestamp(),
   00215:       updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
   00216:       plan: nextPlan,
   00217:     });
   00218:   } else {
   00219:     await setDoc(
   00220:       ref,
   00221:       {
   00222:         updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
   00223:         plan: nextPlan,
   00224:       },
   00225:       { merge: true }
   00226:     );
   00227:   }
   00228: 
   00229:   return { uid, plan: nextPlan, entitlement: computeEntitlement(nextPlan, t) };
   00230: }

   00036: 
   00037: export async function buildStatisticsSnapshot({
   00038:   uf = "PT_RIO",
   00039:   dateFrom,
   00040:   dateTo,
   00041:   scope = "dashboard_full",
   00042:   positions = null,
   00043: } = {}) {
   00044:   if (!dateFrom || !dateTo) {
   00045:     throw new Error("buildStatisticsSnapshot exige dateFrom e dateTo.");
   00046:   }
   00047: 
   00048:   const draws = await getKingResultsByRange({
   00049:     uf,
   00050:     dateFrom,
   00051:     dateTo,
   00052:     positions,
   00053:     mode: "detailed",
   00054:   });
   00055: 
   00056:   const built = buildRanking(draws);
   00057:   const ranking = safeArray(built?.ranking);
   00058:   const rankingScored = applyScoreEngine(ranking);
   00059: 
   00060:   const payload = {
   00061:     version: 1,
   00062:     scope,
   00063:     uf: normalizeUfKey(uf),
   00064:     from: dateFrom,
   00065:     to: dateTo,
>> 00066:     totalDraws: Number(built?.totalDraws || 0),
   00067:     uniqueDays: Number(built?.uniqueDays || 0),
   00068:     totalOcorrencias: Number(built?.totalOcorrencias || 0),
   00069:     ranking,
   00070:     rankingScored,
   00071:     top3: safeArray(rankingScored).slice(0, 3),
   00072:     integrityIssues: safeArray(built?.integrityIssues),
   00073:     countMode: built?.countMode || "byPrize",
   00074:     generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
   00075:     updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
   00076:   };
   00077: 
   00078:   await setDoc(snapshotDocRef(uf, scope), payload, { merge: true });
   00079: 
   00080:   return payload;
   00081: }

   00083: export async function ensureStatisticsSnapshot({
   00084:   uf = "PT_RIO",
   00085:   dateFrom,
   00086:   dateTo,
   00087:   scope = "dashboard_full",
   00088:   force = false,
   00089:   positions = null,
   00090: } = {}) {
   00091:   const current = await readStatisticsSnapshot({ uf, scope });
   00092: 
   00093:   if (!force && current.exists && current.data?.from === dateFrom && current.data?.to === dateTo) {
   00094:     return {
   00095:       source: "cache",
   00096:       data: current.data,
   00097:     };
   00098:   }
   00099: 
   00100:   const data = await buildStatisticsSnapshot({
   00101:     uf,
   00102:     dateFrom,
   00103:     dateTo,
   00104:     scope,
   00105:     positions,
   00106:   });
   00107: 
   00108:   return {
   00109:     source: "rebuilt",
   00110:     data,
   00111:   };
